## 49. Codex

Time: `2026-07-29T22:57:14.934Z` · source line `1060`

```text
Notebook C++ bounded selection уже корректно ограничивает память, но я направил ещё одно ускорение: не allgather’ить все ~50 млн hit-records при `2**21×24`. Каждый rank локально оставляет свой next-K после cursor, а NCCL обменивает только ≤K с каждого rank; глобальный K математически всегда лежит в объединении локальных K. Это сохраняет точность и резко режет PCIe/NCCL трафик без нового CUDA kernel.
```

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

# 
Karing - Simple & Powerful proxy utility

### A singbox GUI based on flutter.

English | 简体中文 | 繁體中文 | 日本語 | 한국어 | Español | Français | Deutsch | Italiano | Tiếng Việt | Türkçe | Русский | فارسی | العربية | Português | Українська | Polski | اردو | Svenska | Norsk | Nederlands | हिन्दी | Ελληνικά | Dansk | বাংলা | ไทย | ਪੰਜਾਬੀ

### Note: Karing has not opened any channel related to Karing on any video platform

## Features

  * Compatible with Clash, V2ray/V2fly, Sing-box, Shadowsocks, Sub, Github Subscriptions.

    * Full `clash` config supported, Partial `clash.meta` config supported.
  * A set of routing rules applied to multiple subscription sources automatically selects efficient nodes.

  * Supports custom routing rule groups and node groups.

    * Customizes default routing rule groups for novice users - ready to use right out of the box.
    * Built-in geo-IP, geo-site, ACL, and other rulesets
  * Backup and synchronization, synchronizing multiple devices with a single configuration.

    * Supports iCloud synchronization [IOS/MacOS].
    * Supports synchronization within the local area network.
    * Supports WebDAV.
    * Supports importing/exporting ZIP files.
  * Built-in support for the modified sing-box core.

  * Introduces a beginner mode for simpler configuration.

  * Plan to support more platforms.

## System Requirements

  * Windows >= 10 (64-bit only)
  * Android >= 8 (arm64-v8a, armeabi-v7a)
  * Linux (64-bit only, glibc >= 2.38 for current .deb packages)
  * IOS >= 15
  * MacOS >= 12 (Intel, Apple Silicon)
  * TvOS >= 17

## Install

  * IOS/TvOS AppStore: (Search Keywords: karing vpn)
    * https://apps.apple.com/us/app/karing/id6472431552
  * IOS/TvOS TestFlight:
    * https://testflight.apple.com/join/RLU59OsJ
  * Android:
    * https://karing.app/download
    * https://github.com/KaringX/karing/releases/latest
    * APKPure https://apkpure.com/p/com.nebula.karing
    * Amazon AppStore https://www.amazon.com/gp/product/B0DJSQDDM8
  * Windows/Macos/Linux:
    * https://karing.app/download
    * https://github.com/KaringX/karing/releases/latest
    * `brew install karing`

[Feature][All] Add Protocol-Based Config Filtering for Subscription Links#1331

Copy link

## Description

FlashWins

opened on Feb 11, 2026

Issue body actions

### 功能描述/Feature description

I request adding the ability to filter configurations by protocol type (VLESS/VMess/Shadowsocks/Trojan/etc.) in subscription links. This feature should be available in two locations:

  * My Profiles > Edit Profile > Filter
  * My Profiles > Add Profiles > Add Profile Link > Filter

Users should be able to select which protocol types they want to include from a subscription link, and only configs matching those protocols will be imported and used for auto-selection.

### 使用场景/Use case

Under severe internet censorship (particularly in Iran and China), certain protocols like Shadowsocks often fail to work in practice, even though they show ping responses during URL tests in the Auto Select feature. --------------------------------------------------------------------------------

# [Feature] Local Inbound Auth & Listener Toggle #1441

New issue

Copy link

New issue

Copy link

Closed

Closed

[Feature] Local Inbound Auth & Listener Toggle#1441

Copy link

## Description

skalbelpori

opened on Apr 8, 2026


### 功能描述/Feature description

Hi!
​I recently came across a technical article on Habr (https://habr.com/ru/articles/1020080/) that highlights a significant security vulnerability in proxy clients.
​Currently, Karing opens inbound ports (SOCKS5/HTTP) without any authentication. This allows third-party apps and "spy" scripts (especially in environments like Android Work Profiles) to easily detect the active proxy by scanning localhost or even use it to analyze traffic patterns. This makes the proxy much easier to detect for DPI and tracking systems.
​Is a fix for this planned? It would be great to have the ability to set a Username/Password for local inbounds to prevent this kind of detection.
Kaing is an amazing client. In Russia this is a new problem right now cause there are rumors that russian apps are going to use this vulnerability to get server IPs and pass this to censors (Roscomnadzor).

### 使用场景/Use case

This can be done directly within the app: just enter your SOCKS5 username and password. This feature is already available in the Husi client.

### 适用系统/Target OS

  * [Input] iOS
  * [Input] macOS
  * [Input] Apple tvOS
  * [Input] Android
  * [Input] Windows
  * [Input] Linux

const int kRemarkMaxLength = 32;

class ServerConfig {
  List<ServerConfigGroupItem> items = [];

  void clear() {
    items = [];
  }

  ServerConfig clone(bool includeDisable, bool includeServers) {
    ServerConfig config = ServerConfig();
    for (var i in items) {
      if (!includeDisable) {
        if (!i.enable) {
          continue;
        }
      }

      config.items.add(i.clone(includeServers: includeServers));
    }
    return config;
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {'items': items};

  void fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>? map) {
    if (map == null) {
      return;
    }

    var tempItems = map["items"] ?? [];

    bool hasCustom = false;
    int index = 1;
    for (var item in tempItems) {
      ServerConfigGroupItem server = ServerConfigGroupItem();
      server.fromJson(item);
      server.index = index++;

      items.add(server);
      if (server.groupid == ServerManager.getCustomGroupId()) {
        hasCustom = true;
      }
    }

  61: func proxyInfo(server *Server, detour adapter.Outbound) *badjson.JSONObject {

  67: 	default:

  71: 	info.Put("name", detour.Tag())

  73: 	delayHistory := server.urlTestHistory.LoadURLTestHistory(adapter.OutboundTag(detour))

  76: 	} else {
  77: 		info.Put("history", []*adapter.URLTestHistory{})
  78: 	}
  79: 	if group, isGroup := detour.(adapter.OutboundGroup); isGroup {
  80: 		info.Put("now", group.Now())
  81: 		info.Put("all", group.All())
  82: 	}
  83: 	return &info
  84: }

  86: func getProxies(server *Server) func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
  87: 	return func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
  88: 		var proxyMap badjson.JSONObject
  89: 		outbounds := common.Filter(server.outbound.Outbounds(), func(detour adapter.Outbound) bool {

  92: 		outbounds = append(outbounds, common.Map(common.Filter(server.endpoint.Endpoints(), func(detour adapter.Endpoint) bool {

 102: 			case C.TypeDirect, C.TypeBlock, C.TypeDNS:

 104: 			}
 105: 			allProxies = append(allProxies, detour.Tag())
 106: 		}
 107: 
 108: 		defaultTag := server.outbound.Default().Tag()
 109: 
 110: 		sort.SliceStable(allProxies, func(i, j int) bool {

 113:

 129: 				tag = detour.Tag()

 132: 		}

 162: func updateProxy(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
 163: 	req := UpdateProxyRequest{}
 164: 	if err := render.DecodeJSON(r.Body, &req); err != nil {
 165: 		render.Status(r, http.StatusBadRequest)
 166: 		render.JSON(w, r, ErrBadRequest)
 167: 		return
 168: 	}
 169: 
 170: 	proxy := r.Context().Value(CtxKeyProxy).(adapter.Outbound)
 171: 	selector, ok := proxy.(*group.Selector)
 172: 	if !ok {
 173: 		render.Status(r, http.StatusBadRequest)
 174: 		render.JSON(w, r, newError("Must be a Selector"))
 175: 		return
 176: 	}
 177: 
 178: 	if !selector.SelectOutbound(req.Name) {
 179: 		render.Status(r, http.StatusBadRequest)
 180: 		render.JSON(w, r, newError("Selector update error: not found"))
 181: 		return
 182: 	}
 183: 
 184: 	render.NoContent(w, r)
 185: }

## 319. Codex

Time: `2026-07-29T23:02:41.047Z` · source line `6167`

````text
Нашёл точный механизм.

- `PUT /proxies/GLOBAL` не поддерживается: `GLOBAL` — синтетическая dashboard-запись типа `Fallback`, а не настоящий outbound, поэтому 404 корректен.
- Поддержан только выбор у настоящей группы типа `Selector`:

```http
PUT http://127.0.0.1:3057/proxies/<selector-tag>
Content-Type: application/json

{"name":"<outbound-tag>"}
```

- Для не-`Selector` будет `400 Must be a Selector`.
- При текущем `GLOBAL: Fallback, now=direct_out` поддерживаемого control-API для замены default-outbound нет. Нужен реальный `Selector` в `/proxies`; иначе переключение возможно только через штатный UI с полным reload конфигурации.

Исходники и строки отправлены контроллеру; runtime, UI и конфиг не менял.
````

  210:   static Future<ReturnResultError?> _restoreBackup(
  211:     BuildContext context,
  212:     Uri uri,
  213:   ) async {
  214:     if (PlatformUtils.isPC()) {
  215:       await windowManager.show();
  216:     }
  217:     String? url = uri.queryParameters["url"];
  218:     if (url != null) {
  219:       try {
  220:         url = Uri.decodeComponent(url);
  221:       } catch (err) {}
  222:     }
  223:     if (url == null || url.isEmpty) {
  224:       return ReturnResultError("decode query param url failed");
  225:     }
  226:     if (!context.mounted) {
  227:       return ReturnResultError("page unmounted");
  228:     }
  229:     return GroupHelper.restoreBackupFromUrl(context, url);
  230:   }
  231: 
  232:   static Future<ReturnResultError?> addConfigBySubscriptionLink(
  233:     BuildContext context,
  234:     String urlOrContent,
  235:     String? name,
  236:     String? ispUser,
  237:     RemoteISPConfig? ispConfig,
  238:     bool autoAdd,
  239:     bool? xhwid,
  240:     List<String> outboundDns,
  241:   ) async {
  242:     ServerConfigGroupItem? item = ServerManager.getGroupByUrlOrPath(
  243:       urlOrContent,
  244:     );
  245:     if (item != null) {
  246:       Log.w("$urlOrContent already exists, skip add");
  247:       return null;
  248:     }
  249:     int kMaxPush = 1;
  250:     if (AddProfileByLinkOrContentScreen.pushed >= kMaxPush) {
  251:       return ReturnResultError("addprofile request already exists");
  252:     }
  253:     UrlLauncherUtils.closeWebview();
  254:     if (!context.mounted) {
  255:       return null;
  256:     }
  257: 
  258:     bool? ok = await Navigator.push(
  259:       context,
  260:       MaterialPageRoute(
  261:         settings: AddProfileByLinkOrContentScreen.routSettings(),
  262:         builder: (context) => AddProfileByLinkOrContentScreen(
  263:           urlOrContent: urlOrContent,
  264:           name: name,
  265:           ispId: ispConfig?.id,
  266:           ispUser: ispUser,
  267:           autoAdd: autoAdd,
  268:           xhwid: xhwid,
  269:           outboundDns: outboundDns,
  270:         ),
  271:       ),
  272:     );
  273:     if (ok != true) {
  274:       return ReturnResultError("addprofile failed or canceled by user");
  275:     }
  276: 
  277:     return null;
  278:   }
  279: }

 1081:         ReturnResultError? err = await start("launch");
 1082:         if (err == null) {
 1083:           MoveToBackgroundUtils.moveToBackground(
 1084:             duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
 1085:           );
 1086:         }
 1087:       } else if (command == "disconnect") {
 1088:         await stop();
 1089:         MoveToBackgroundUtils.moveToBackground(
 1090:           duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
 1091:         );
 1092:       } else if (command == "reconnect") {
 1093:         await stop();
 1094:         ReturnResultError? err = await start("launch");
 1095:         if (err == null) {
 1096:           MoveToBackgroundUtils.moveToBackground(
 1097:             duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
 1098:           );
 1099:         }
 1100:       }
 1101:     }
 1102:     if (_initUrl.isNotEmpty) {
 1103:       await SchemeHandler.handle(context, _initUrl);
 1104:       _initUrl = "";
 1105:     }
 1106: 
 1107:     setState(() {});
 1108:   }
 1109: 
 1110:   Future<void> _vpnConnect(String from, bool background) async {
 1111:     Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 0), () async {
 1112:       ReturnResultError? error = await start(from);
 1113:       if (error == null) {
 1114:         if (background) {
 1115:           MoveToBackgroundUtils.moveToBackground(
 1116:             duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
 1117:           );
 1118:         }
 1119:       }
 1120:     });
 1121:   }
 1122: 
 1123:   Future<void> _vpnDisconnect(String from, bool background) async {
 1124:     Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 0), () async {
 1125:       await stop();
 1126:       if (background) {
 1127:         MoveToBackgroundUtils.moveToBackground(
 1128:           duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
 1129:         );
 1130:       }
 1131:     });
 1132:   }
 1133: 
 1134:   Future<void> _vpnReconnect(String from, bool background) async {
 1135:     Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 0), () async {
 1136:       await stop();
 1137:       ReturnResultError? error = await start(from);
 1138:       if (error == null) {
 1139:         if (background) {
 1140:           MoveToBackgroundUtils.moveToBackground(

 1142:           );

 1144:       }
 1145:     });
 1146:   }

 1148:   Future<ReturnResultError?> _onRequestStartVPN(String from) async {
 1149:     if (ServerManager.getUpdateDirty()) {
 1150:       ServerManager.setDirty(true);
 1151:     }
 1152:     if (_state == FlutterVpnServiceState.connected) {
 1153:       return await checkAndReload(from, disableShowAlertDialog: true);
 1154:     }
 1155:     if (_state == FlutterVpnServiceState.disconnected) {
 1156:       return await start(from, disableShowAlertDialog: true);
 1157:     }
 1158:     return null;
 1159:   }

ou can use the system hotspot to share the network (this method requires your device to have a wireless network card). Go to System - Windows Settings - Network and Internet - Mobile Hotspot - Enable 'Share my Internet connection with other devices' and set the 'Network Name' (e.g., My Hotpot) and 'Network Password'. Then, go to Network and Internet - Status - Change adapter options - Right-click 'Karing TUN Network Adapter' - Properties - Sharing - Check 'Allow other network users to connect through this computer's Internet connection'. Locate the network adapter labeled 'Microsoft Wi-Fi Direct Virtual Adapter #xxx' and select it - Click 'OK'. Other mobile devices can then connect to the hotspot (e.g., the one named 'My Hotpot') that you've configured. Note that if karingservice.exe appears after enabling the hotspot... For the issue of extremely high CPU usage, please disable 'Automatic Routing' in karing-tun.

## 350. Codex

Time: `2026-07-30T03:10:24.807Z` · source line `6237`

```text
Штатного CLI/deep-link/IPC для выбора конкретного proxy в Karing нет.

Поддерживаются только:

- `karing://connect`
- `karing://disconnect`
- `karing://reconnect`
- импорт конфигурации, восстановление backup и Apple TV actions.

`karing://reconnect?background=true` перезапускает уже сохранённое подключение, но не принимает `server`, `tag` или `name`; лишние параметры игнорируются. Named selection остаётся только UI-потоком: результат `ServerSelectScreen` → обновление `selectDefault` → `setServerAndReload`.

Локальные HTTP-серверы Karing относятся к dashboard, LAN backup sync и proxy cluster — management endpoint выбора сервера среди них отсутствует. Runtime, UI и конфиги не менял.
```
